Even if Seattle is a 60% favorite in each game, the compounded probability of winning 4 straight is ~13%. The 19.1% price implies Seattle is being overvalued, likely due to recency bias from recent performance. Vegas Super Bowl futures typically have favorites around 10-15%, making 19.1% for a non-dominant team appear inflated. | Key factors: Super Bowl requires winning 3-4 consecutive playoff games, Compounding playoff win probabilities significantly reduces overall odds, No whale activity or smart money tracking this market | Risks: News headline suggests strong recent performance boosting legitimate odds, Without current Vegas odds comparison, true probability is uncertain
